\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\left\langle h_{xx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)),\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)\right\rangle,

where in (
C.10
)
\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)
,
\mathcal{Y}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),\,\mathcal{Z}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)

is the solution of (
7.10
).
Thanks to (
4.19
) we can check, by estimates similar to those
already done, that

(C.11)

||\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)||,\ ||\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)||,\ ||\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)||\leq C_{T}||\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}||.

Now
\mathcal{X}\mapsto\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)

are linear. Indeed, taking an initial condition
\alpha\mathcal{X}_{1}+\beta\mathcal{X}_{2}
,
one can use the
\sigma
-algebras
\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{1\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{2\cdot}t}^{s}

and the linearity follows easily. Therefore the maps
\mathcal{X}\mapsto\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)
,
\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)
,

\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)
belong to
\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{H}_{m},\mathcal{H}_{m}).

Define

u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s):=\dfrac{u_{X+\epsilon\mathcal{X},t}(s)-u_{Xt}(s)}{\varepsilon},\,X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s):=\dfrac{X_{X+\epsilon\mathcal{X},t}(s)-X_{Xt}(s)}{\varepsilon},

(C.12)

Z_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s):=\dfrac{Z_{X+\epsilon\mathcal{X},t}(s)-Z_{Xt}(s)}{\epsilon}\ ,r_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s):=\dfrac{r_{X+\epsilon\mathcal{X},t}^{j}(s)-r_{Xt}^{j}(s)}{\epsilon},

\rho_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s):=\dfrac{\rho_{X+\epsilon\mathcal{X},t}^{j}(s)-\rho_{Xt}^{j}(s)}{\epsilon},

then we have the relations:

(C.13)

X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)=\mathcal{X}+\int_{t}^{s}u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(\tau)d\tau,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\bigg[l_{vx}\left(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\right)X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

(C.14)

\displaystyle\qquad+l_{vv}\left(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\right)u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\bigg]d\theta+Z_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)=0,

\displaystyle-dZ_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\bigg[l_{xx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

(C.15)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\,+l_{xv}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\bigg]d\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,\,+\int_{0}^{1}D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left(((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(X_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{\epsilon}(s))d\theta

\displaystyle\quad\,\,-\sum_{j}r_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)dw_{j}(s)-\sum_{j}\rho_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)db_{j}(s),

\displaystyle Z_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{\epsilon}(T)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}h_{xx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T))X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T)d\theta

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left(((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T))\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(X_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{\epsilon}(T))d\theta.

By techniques already used, we can check that
u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),

X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),Z_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

are bounded in
L_{\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}}^{\infty}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})

and
r_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s),\rho_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)

are bounded in
L_{\mathcal{F}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m}).

We consider then subsequences of
u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),

Z_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),r_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)
,

\rho_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)
which converge weakly
in
L_{\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})

to
\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{R}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j}(s),\Theta_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j}(s).

It follows immediately that

(C.16)

\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)=\mathcal{X}+\int_{t}^{s}\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(\tau)d\tau.

Define

\displaystyle L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s):=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\bigg[l_{vx}\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

\displaystyle\qquad+l_{vv}\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\bigg]d\theta.

We want to show that

(C.17)

L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\rightarrow L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)=l_{vx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)+l_{vv}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)

in
L_{\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})

weakly as
\epsilon\rightarrow 0
. Let us take
\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)
in
L_{\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m}).

We have:

\displaystyle\quad\int_{t}^{T}\langle L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),\Gamma_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\rangle ds

\displaystyle=\int_{t}^{T}\left\langle X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),\int_{0}^{1}l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)d\theta\right\rangle ds

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{t}^{T}\left\langle u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),\int_{0}^{1}l_{vv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)d\theta\right\rangle ds.

Since
X_{Xt}(\cdot)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(\cdot)\rightarrow X_{Xt}(\cdot)
,
\ u_{Xt}(\cdot)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(\cdot)\rightarrow u_{Xt}(\cdot)

in
L_{\mathcal{F}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})
, and

l_{xx}
is continuous and bounded by assumptions (
4.5
),
(
4.7
), it follows by classical Lebesgue integral theory
that

\int_{0}^{1}l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)d\theta\rightarrow l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\ \text{ in}\ L_{\mathcal{F}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m}),

and a similar argument applies to
\int_{0}^{1}l_{vv}(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)d\theta.

Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{t}^{T}\Big\langle L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big\rangle ds\rightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{t}^{T}\Big\langle\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),l_{xv}(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s))\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big\rangle ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t}^{T}\Big\langle\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),l_{vv}(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s))\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big\rangle ds.

Transposing the matrices
l_{xv}
and
l_{vv}
we obtain

\int_{t}^{T}\Big\langle L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big\rangle ds\rightarrow\int_{t}^{T}\Big\langle L_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\Gamma_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big\rangle ds,

and since
\Gamma_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)
is arbitrary, the result (
C.17
)
follows. So (
C.14
) yields:

(C.18)

l_{vx}(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s))\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)+l_{vv}(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s))\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)+\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)=0.

A similar argument implies:

(C.19)

\displaystyle\quad\int_{0}^{1}\bigg[l_{xx}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\bigg]d\theta

\displaystyle\rightarrow l_{xx}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)+l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\ \ \text{in }L_{\mathcal{F}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})\ \text{weakly}.

Now, the property (
3.13
) holds true, so we can also state
that

\displaystyle\quad\int_{0}^{1}D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left(((X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))d\theta

(C.20)

\displaystyle\rightarrow D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s))\ \text{in }L_{\mathcal{F}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})\ \text{weakly}.

Also,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{0}^{1}h_{xx}(X_{Xt}(T)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T))X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T)d\theta+\int_{0}^{1}D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left(((X_{Xt}(T)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T))\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(T))d\theta

(C.21)

\displaystyle\rightarrow h_{xx}(X_{Xt}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T))

weakly in the subspace of
\mathcal{H}_{m}
of
\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{T}
-measurable variable.
From relation (
C.15
), by conditioning and letting
\epsilon\rightarrow 0

we obtain

(C.22)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{s}^{T}\bigg(l_{xx}(X_{Xt}(\tau),u_{Xt}(\tau))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(\tau)+l_{xv}(X_{Xt}(\tau),u_{Xt}(\tau))\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(\tau)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(\tau)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{\tau}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(\tau))\bigg)d\tau

\displaystyle\quad\,+h_{xx}(X_{Xt}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T))\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{s}\Bigg].

This implies that
\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)
is an
Itô process, and satisfies

(C.23)

\left\{\begin{aligned} -d\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)&=\bigg[l_{xx}(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)+l_{xv}(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s))\mathcal{\mathcal{U}}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\\
&\qquad+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s))\bigg]ds\\
&\quad\,-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathcal{R}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j}(s)dw_{j}(s)-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Theta_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j}(s)db_{j}(s),\\
\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)&=h_{xx}(X_{Xt}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)).\end{aligned}\right.

We can also check that
\mathcal{R}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j}(s),\Theta_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j}(s)

are the weak limits of
r_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)
,
\rho_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{j,\epsilon}(s)
.

Since we have also

\displaystyle\Big\langle\mathcal{X},Z_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(t)\Big\rangle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\langle\mathcal{X},\int_{0}^{1}\bigg[l_{xx}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s),u_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\Big)u_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s)\bigg]d\theta

\displaystyle\qquad\,\,+\int_{0}^{1}D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left(((X_{Xt}(s)+\theta\epsilon X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(X_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(s))d\theta\Bigg\rangle,

we can state that

(C.24)

\Big\langle\mathcal{X},Z_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}^{\epsilon}(t)\Big\rangle\rightarrow\Big\langle\mathcal{X},\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(t)\Big\rangle.

Since
Z_{Xt}(t)=D_{X}V(X\otimes m,t),
from the definition of the
second derivative (see (
2.38
)), we obtain:

(C.25)

D_{X}^{2}V(X\otimes m,t)(\mathcal{X})=\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(t).

We then proceed to prove the continuity property (
7.9
).
By definition,

(C.26)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)=\mathcal{X}_{k}+\int_{t_{k}}^{s}\mathcal{\mathcal{U}}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(\tau)d\tau,\ s>t_{k},

(C.27)

\displaystyle-d\mathcal{\ Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)=\Bigg[l_{xx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)+l_{xv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{\mathcal{U}}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s))\Bigg]ds

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathcal{R}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}^{j}(s)dw_{j}(s)-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Theta_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}^{j}(s)db_{j}(s),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(T)=h_{xx}(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(T)),

(C.28)

\displaystyle l_{vx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)+l_{vv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)+\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(s)=0,

and

(C.29)

\displaystyle X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)=X_{k}+\int_{t_{k}}^{s}u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(\tau)d\tau+\sigma(w(s)-w(t_{k}))+\beta(b(s)-b(t_{k})),

\displaystyle-dZ_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)=\left(l_{x}(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s))+D_{X}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{s}}\right)\right)\right)ds

(C.30)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\,\,\,-\sum_{j=1}^{n}r_{X_{k}t_{k}}^{j}(s)dw_{j}(s)-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\rho_{X_{k}t_{k}}^{j}(s)db_{j}(s),

\displaystyle Z_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T)=h_{x}(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T))+D_{X}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((Y_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{T}}\right)\right),

(C.31)

\displaystyle l_{v}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)+Z_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)=0.

We need to prove that

(C.32)

\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(t_{k})\rightarrow\mathcal{Z}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(t).

We fix
s>t.
We can assume that
s>t_{k}.
Since, from (
C.11
),

(C.33)

||\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}(t_{k})-\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(t_{k})||\leq C_{T}||\mathcal{X}_{k}-\mathcal{X}||,

it is sufficient to prove that

(C.34)

\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(t_{k})\rightarrow\mathcal{Z}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(t).

So we have to consider the system:

(C.35)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)=\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}+\int_{t_{k}}^{s}\mathcal{U}{}_{X\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(\tau)d\tau,\ s>t_{k},

\displaystyle-d\mathcal{\ Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)=\Bigg[l_{xx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)+l_{xv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{\mathcal{U}}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{B_{t_{k}}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s))\Bigg]ds

(C.36)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathcal{R}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}^{j}(s)dw_{j}(s)-\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Theta_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}_{k}t_{k}}^{j}(s)db_{j}(s),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(T)=h_{xx}(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(T)+D_{X}^{2}F_{T}\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{T}}\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(T)),

(C.37)

\displaystyle l_{vx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)+l_{vv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)+\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)=0.

From the proof of Proposition
5.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle||X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)-X_{Xt}(s)||

\displaystyle\leq||X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)-X_{Xt_{k}}(s)||+||X_{Xt_{k}}(s)-X_{Xt}(s)||

(C.38)

\displaystyle\leq C_{T}||X_{k}-X||+C_{T}||X||(t_{k}-t)+C_{T}(t_{k}-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}

and similar estimates for
||Z_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)-Z_{Xt}(s)||
and

||u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)-u_{Xt}(s)||
. We next introduce the
\sigma
-algebras

(C.39)

\mathcal{\widetilde{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{s}=\underset{\{j|t_{j}<s\}}{\cup}\mathcal{F}_{X_{j}\mathcal{X}t_{j}}^{s}\cup\mathcal{F}_{t}^{t_{j}}.

Note that
X
is
\mathcal{\widetilde{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{s}
-measurable,
so
\mathcal{\widetilde{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{s}
is an extension
of
\mathcal{F}_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{s}.
To define the processes
\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)
,
\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)
,
\mathcal{\ Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)
for

t<s<t_{k}
, we set

(C.40)

\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)=\mathcal{X},\ \mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)=0,\ \mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)=\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(t_{k})|\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}^{s}],\ t<s<t_{k}.

To simplify notation, we shall denote
\mathcal{X}^{k}(s)=\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)

,
\mathcal{U}^{k}(s)=

\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)
,\ \mathcal{Z}^{k}(s)=\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)
.
We first see that
\mathcal{X}^{k}(s)
,
\mathcal{U}^{k}(s)
and

\mathcal{Z}^{k}(s)
remain bounded in
L_{\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{\infty}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m}).

We pick a subsequence of
\mathcal{X}^{k}(s),\mathcal{U}^{k}(s),\mathcal{Z}^{k}(s)
,
which converges weakly to
\mathcal{\widetilde{X}}(\cdot),\mathcal{\widetilde{U}}(\cdot),
\widetilde{\mathcal{Z}}(\cdot)

in
L_{\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m}).

Then

\mathcal{\widetilde{X}}(s)\ =\mathcal{X}+\int_{t}^{s}\mathcal{\widetilde{U}}(\tau)d\tau.

As for the proof of (
C.17
)-(
C.21
) we have

\displaystyle\quad\,l_{xx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)+l_{xv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{\mathcal{U}}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s)

\displaystyle\quad\,+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{\mathcal{X}}t_{k}}(s))

\displaystyle\rightarrow l_{xx}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{\widetilde{X}}(s)+l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{\widetilde{U}}(s)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{Xt}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{\widetilde{X}}(s))

weakly in
L_{\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}}^{2}(t,T;\mathcal{H}_{m})
, and likewise for similar terms. We have used here the property

(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{s}}=(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}

since
\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}=\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{s}\cup\mathcal{B}_{t}^{t_{k}}

and
X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)
is independent of
\mathcal{B}_{t}^{t_{k}}.

Necessarily
\mathcal{\widetilde{X}}(s)=\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),\ \mathcal{\widetilde{U}}(s)=\mathcal{\mathcal{U}}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),\ \widetilde{\mathcal{Z}}(s)=\mathcal{Z}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s).

We need next to prove the strong convergence. We first check the
relation that

\displaystyle I_{k}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{k}}^{T}\Bigg[\Big\langle l_{xx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)\ \Big\rangle+2\Big\langle l_{xv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)\ \Big\rangle

\displaystyle+\Big\langle l_{vv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s),\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)\ \Big\rangle+\Big\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)\Big\rangle\Bigg]ds

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle h_{xx}(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t_{k}}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\mathcal{Z}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(t_{k}),\mathcal{X}\Big\rangle

converges to

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\mathcal{Z}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(t),\mathcal{X}\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\int_{t}^{T}\Bigg[\bigg\langle l_{xx}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\ \bigg\rangle+2\bigg\langle l_{xv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\ \bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle l_{vv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s),\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\ \bigg\rangle+\bigg\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{Xt}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)),\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\bigg\rangle\Bigg]ds

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle h_{xx}(X_{Xt}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{Xt}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)),\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)\bigg\rangle.

From this convergence and weak convergence, we obtain immediately

\displaystyle J_{k}=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{k}}^{T}\Bigg[\bigg\langle l_{xx}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\ \bigg\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+2\bigg\langle l_{xv}\Big(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s),u_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\Big)(\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\ \bigg\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\langle l_{vv}\Big(X_{Xt}(s),u_{Xt}(s)\Big)(\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)),\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\ \bigg\rangle

(C.41)

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)),\mathcal{X}{}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\bigg\rangle\Bigg]ds

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle h_{xx}(X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T))(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T))

\displaystyle\qquad+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{k}t_{k}}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)\cdot(\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\ 0.

But

(C.42)

\displaystyle J_{k}\geq

\displaystyle\lambda\int_{t_{k}}^{T}\left\lVert\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)\right\rVert^{2}ds-(c^{\prime}_{l}+c^{\prime})\int_{t_{k}}^{T}||\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2}ds

\displaystyle-(c^{\prime}_{h}+c^{\prime}_{T})||\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)||^{2},

and

(C.43)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(T)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(T)||^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\epsilon)T\int_{t_{k}}^{T}||\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\epsilon}\right)(t_{k}-t)^{2}\sup_{s}||\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{t_{k}}^{T}||\mathcal{X}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{X}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2}ds\leq

\displaystyle(1+\epsilon)\dfrac{T^{2}}{2}\int_{t_{k}}^{T}||\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\epsilon}\right)(t_{k}-t)^{2}\sup_{s}||\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2}.

From the assumption (
4.19
), since
t_{k}\downarrow t
and

J_{k}\rightarrow 0
, we obtain
\int_{t_{k}}^{T}||\mathcal{U}_{X_{k}\mathcal{X}t_{k}}(s)-\mathcal{U}_{X\mathcal{X}t}(s)||^{2}ds\rightarrow 0.

It is then easy to get (
C.32
). This concludes the proof
of Proposition
7.3
.

C.4.
PROOF OF THEOREM
7.5

From the optimality principle we can write

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\,\dfrac{1}{\epsilon}\int_{s}^{s+\epsilon}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}l\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(\tau),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(\tau)\Big)dm(x)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(\tau)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{\tau}}\right)\right)\right]d\tau

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{\epsilon}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s+\epsilon)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s+\epsilon}},t+\epsilon\right)\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{Xt}(s)\otimes m,s)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)\bigg].

From the continuity of functions
s\mapsto X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)
,
the first term converges to
\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}l(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))dm(x)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(F((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}})\right)
.
Therefore we have

(C.44)

\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}l\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)dm(x)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)+\dfrac{d}{ds}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)=0.

But we have proven that the function
X\mapsto\mathbb{E}\left(V((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s)\right)

satisfies all the conditions for the applicability of Theorem
3.2
.
So

(C.45)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{ds}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)=

\displaystyle\dfrac{\partial}{\partial s}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle D_{X}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bigg\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)(\sigma N^{j}_{s}),\sigma N^{j}_{s}\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\beta^{2}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bigg\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)(e^{j}),e^{j}\bigg\rangle,\,\text{a.e.}\ s\in(0,T),

where
N^{j}_{s}
are scalar standard Gaussian independent of
\mathcal{F}_{Xt}^{s}.
Recall
that
D_{X}\mathbb{E}\left(V((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s)\right)=Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)

and

\Big\langle Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big\rangle=\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\cdot u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)dm(x)\right).

Since

l_{v}(X_{\cdot,t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))+Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)=0,

we can write

(C.46)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}l\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)dm(x)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\cdot u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)dm(x)\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}H\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)dm(x)\right).

Therefore (
C.44
) and (
C.45
) yields

(C.47)

\displaystyle\dfrac{\partial}{\partial s}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}H\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)dm(x)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)(\sigma N_{s}),\sigma N_{s}\bigg\rangle+\dfrac{\beta^{2}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bigg\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}},s\right)\right)(e^{j}),e^{j}\bigg\rangle=0,\text{a.e.}.

Also,

(C.48)

\mathbb{E}\left(V\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}},T\right)\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}h(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T))dm(x)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m,T)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right).

Taking
s=t
in (
C.47
) and
t=T
in (
C.48
), and
recalling that
Z_{X_{\cdot}t}(t)=D_{X}V(X_{\cdot}\otimes m,t),
we obtain
(
7.33
). Now if a functional
V(X\otimes m,t)
satisfies
the regularity properties of the value function, then it satisfies
also (
C.47
) and (
C.48
). Then (
C.44
) holds
and integrating with respect to
s
, between
t
and
T,
we obtain

(C.49)

V(X_{\cdot}\otimes m,t)=J_{X_{\cdot}t}(u_{X_{\cdot}t}(\cdot)).

Since the right-hand side is the value function and uniquely defined,
the solution is necessarily unique. Note that
X_{\cdot}
must be independent
of
\mathcal{F}_{t}.
But given
X_{\cdot}
, we can always construct
the Wiener process, so that this condition is satisfied. This concludes
the proof.

Appendix D
PROOF OF PROPOSITION
8.1

Considering Proposition
7.3
, we can enlarge the space
of controls as follows: Let
\mathcal{B}_{x}
be a family of
\sigma
-algebras independent of the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{t}
and
X_{\cdot}
, which is the initial condition of the system (
4.20
)
be
\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}
-measurable. Let also
\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}
be also

\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}
-measurable. We consider the control problem

(D.1)

\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s)=\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}+\int_{t}^{s}\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\tau)d\tau,

(D.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}t}(\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\cdot))=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{t}^{T}\Bigg[\bigg\langle l_{xx}\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s)),\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s)\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+2\bigg\langle l_{xv}\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s),\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s)\bigg\rangle+\bigg\langle l_{vv}\Big(X_{X_{{}_{\cdot}}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s),\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(s)\bigg\rangle\Bigg]ds

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle h_{xx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T))\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(T)+D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(T)),\mathcal{X}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(T)\bigg\rangle.

Then we have

(D.3)

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\cdot)}\mathcal{J}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}t}(\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\cdot))=\dfrac{1}{2}\Big\langle\mathcal{Z}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(t),\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\Big\langle D_{X}^{2}V(X_{\cdot}\otimes m,t)(\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}),\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}\Big\rangle=\mathcal{J}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}t}(\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(\cdot)).

From this formula and the uniqueness of the point of minimum, we can
give a formula for
\langle D_{X}\langle D_{X}^{2}V(X_{\cdot}\otimes m,t)(\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}),\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}\rangle,\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}\rangle

where
\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}
is also
\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}
-measurable and
independent of
\mathcal{F}_{t}.
This formula can be applied to

\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}=\sigma N_{t}
and
e^{j}
, respectively. This formula
will be valid thanks to the assumptions (
8.16
), (
8.17
), (
8.18
), (
8.19
). This provides the justification needed to obtain the master equation
(
8.1
). Denoting by
D_{X_{\cdot}}\mathcal{J}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}t}(\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\cdot))

the G
\textroundcap
ateaux differential of
\mathcal{J}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}t}(\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\cdot))

with respect to
X_{\cdot}
, when
\mathcal{V}_{\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}(\cdot)

is fixed, then we have

(D.4)

\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle D_{X}\Big\langle D_{X}^{2}V(X_{\cdot}\otimes m,t)(\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}),\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}\Big\rangle,\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}\bigg\rangle=D_{X_{\cdot}}\mathcal{J}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}t}(\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(\cdot)).

We get the very long formula:

(D.5)

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle D_{X}\Big\langle D_{X}^{2}V(X_{\cdot}\otimes m,t)(\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}),\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}\Big\rangle,\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{t}^{T}\left[\bigg\langle\ \left(l_{xxx}\Big(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s)\right)\,\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle\right.

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle\ \left(D_{X}^{3}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s))\right)\,(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle\ \Big(l_{xxv}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\,\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle\ \Big(l_{xvx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\,\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\bigg\langle\ \Big(l_{xvv}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\,\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle\ \Big(l_{vvx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\,\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle\left.+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle\ \Big(l_{vvv}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s),u_{X_{\cdot}t}(s))\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s)\Big)\,\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s),\mathcal{U}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(s)\,\bigg\rangle\right]ds

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle\ \Big(h_{xxx}(X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T))\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(T)\Big)\,\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)\,\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigg\langle\ \left(D_{X}^{3}\mathbb{E}\left(F_{T}\left((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(T)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{T}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(T))\right)\,(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)),\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{X}_{\cdot}t}(T)\,\bigg\rangle.

This formula is not easy to read. One must keep in mind that
D_{X}^{3}\mathbb{E}\left(F((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}})\right)

\in\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{H}_{m};\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{H}_{m};\mathcal{H}_{m}))
, hence

\left(D_{X}^{3}\mathbb{E}\left(F((X_{X_{\cdot}t}(s)\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}_{t}^{s}})\right)(\mathcal{X}_{X_{\cdot}\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}t}(s))\right)\in\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{H}_{m};\mathcal{H}_{m}).

The interpretation of the other terms is similar. More specifically, we can express

\displaystyle\quad\bigg\langle D_{X}^{3}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)\right)(\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot})(Y_{\cdot}),Y_{\cdot}\bigg\rangle=\bigg\langle D_{X}\Big\langle D_{X}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(F\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)\right)(Y_{\cdot}),Y_{\cdot}\Big\rangle,\mathcal{Z}_{\cdot}\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(D^{3}\dfrac{dF}{d\nu}\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)(Z_{x})\mathcal{Z}_{x}\right)Y_{x}\cdot Y_{x}dm(x)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left(\ \mathbb{E}^{\mathcal{B}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbb{E}^{1\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(D_{1}^{2}\dfrac{d^{2}F}{d\nu^{2}}\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)(Z_{x},Z_{x^{1}}^{1})\mathcal{Z}_{x^{1}}^{1}\right)Y_{x}\cdot Y_{x}dm(x^{1})\right)dm(x)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left(\ \mathbb{E}^{\mathcal{B}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbb{E}^{1\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(D^{2}D_{1}\dfrac{d^{2}F}{d\nu^{2}}\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)(Z_{x},Z_{x^{1}}^{1})\mathcal{Z}_{x}\right)Y_{x^{1}}^{1}\cdot Y_{x}dm(x^{1})\right)dm(x)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left(\ \mathbb{E}^{\mathcal{B}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbb{E}^{1\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(DD_{1}^{2}\dfrac{d^{2}F}{d\nu^{2}}\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)(Z_{x},Z_{x^{1}}^{1})\mathcal{Z}_{x^{1}}^{1}\right)Y_{x^{1}}^{1}\cdot Y_{x}dm(x^{1})\right)dm(x)\right)\right)

(D.6)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left(\ \mathbb{E}^{\mathcal{B}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbb{E}^{1\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbb{E}^{2\mathcal{B}_{\cdot}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(DD_{1}D_{2}\dfrac{d^{3}F}{d\nu^{3}}\left((Z_{\cdot}\otimes m)^{\mathcal{B}}\right)(Z_{x},Z_{x^{1}}^{1},Z_{x^{2}}^{2})\mathcal{Z}_{x^{2}}^{2}\right)Y_{x^{1}}^{1}\cdot Y_{x}dm(x^{2})\right)dm(x^{1})\right)dm(x)\right)\right).

Thanks to (
D.6
) and assumptions (
8.16
), (
8.17
), (
8.18
), (
8.19
),
we can see that formula (
D.5
) is valid. This completes
the proof.
